# Camera-ready numbers

Generated by `scripts/camera_ready_numbers.py` on 2026-10-21 17:01. Every value below was recomputed from the raw run records; full precision is in `camera_ready_numbers.json`.

Inputs: `results` = `results`; `audit` = `records/standard_threshold_audit.json`; `passive` = `sim-records/all100_passive.json`; `settle` = `sim-records/settle.json`; `probe_dir` = `sim-records/probes`; `shoot` = `sim-records/sk_shoot.json`; `search` = `sim-records/vt10_search.json`; `xml_dir` = `assets/unimals_100/xml`; `gait_traces` = `gait-traces`; `logs` = `results`; `review_panels` = `review-panels`.

The zero-action, probe and random-shooting records (`passive`, `settle`, `probe_dir`, `shoot`, `search`) were made on 2026-10-05 and ship in the dataset under `sim-records/`. The MuJoCo re-run (`physics.*` rows) already recomputes the zero-action counts and steps from the XMLs. The `logs.*` rows read the kept training logs (`logs.zip` of the dataset) and the `panels.*` rows the per-cell tables of the review-period panels (`panels.zip`). Run time 630.8 s on CPU; package versions are in the JSON.

Conventions. Rules are Reset-free (tau = 0), Standard (tau = 0.20; keys write it len, after a draft name) and Strict (tau = 0.50), in ascending tau. Rankings use three-seed mean PSR (per-step reward) under the common evaluator (tau_eval = 0.20) unless the evaluator column says otherwise. Intervals are 95% percentile intervals from B = 10,000 body resamples drawn with numpy default_rng(42) (one resample matrix per body set, shared by every statistic on that set), conditional on the three seeds. Permutation tests use B = 10,000 draws from a fresh default_rng(20260923) per test and p = (1 + #null at or beyond observed)/(B + 1). Retention is the mean over bodies of the per-body ratio of three-seed means. The post hoc column says "yes" for analyses added after review, "no (reviewed version)" for analyses reported in the reviewed version without a prior decision rule, and "no (planned)" for tests whose rule was fixed in advance. Body sets defined by outcomes (the 89, 77, 68, 65 and 90) are post hoc sensitivity sets.

## Comparison with the authors' earlier summaries

414 values from the results summary and the draft text that the paper was written from were checked: 379 match at the printed precision, 12 match in the point value but differ in an interval endpoint, 23 differ, and 0 were not computed.

Expected values are shown at their printed precision and computed values with one more decimal. For a value with an interval both are given as [value, lower, upper]. Differences marked Monte Carlo come from a different random stream, not from different data or definitions: with B = 10,000 a permutation p near 0.005 has a Monte Carlo standard error of about 0.0007, and bootstrap interval endpoints move by about 0.01. The paper prints this script's values.

## Upper height bound (run summaries, post hoc)

| key | value | CI | body set | evaluator | definition | post hoc |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `ub.corpus.controllers_ended` | 1 |  | 900 corpus runs | neutral (no fall clause) | Corpus controllers with at least one neutral-evaluator episode shorter than the 1,000-step limit. The neutral evaluator has no fall clause, so only the upper height bound max(3 z0, 2 m) can end its episodes early. | yes |
| `ub.corpus.ended_ids` | [floor-5506-10-6-01-15-48-35 (Strict, seed 44)] |  | 900 corpus runs | neutral (no fall clause) | Those controllers (body, training rule, seed). | yes |
| `ub.corpus.episodes_ended` | [5] |  | 900 corpus runs | neutral (no fall clause) | Episodes of each that ended before the limit, of its 20 neutral-evaluator episodes (fall_rate x 20). | yes |
| `ub.corpus.common_equals_neutral` | [yes] |  | 900 corpus runs | common vs neutral | Whether the common-evaluator record of each equals its neutral record field by field. The evaluators share their random streams, so equal records mean that the same episodes ended under both. | yes |
| `ub.no_time_limit.training_ends` | 0 |  | 90 runs without a time limit |  | Training-episode ends summed over the Reset-free runs without a time limit (implementation_reset_count, every episode end over all environments and updates). These runs have no fall clause and a step limit beyond the budget, so only the upper height bound could end an episode. | yes |

## Printed numbers this script does not recompute

| numbers | where they come from |
|---|---|
| Review-period panels (Humanoid fall rules and time limits, Ant reset initialization and breadth, Evolution Gym timeouts): the bootstrap intervals of rho, the top-10 overlaps, the counts of bodies with negative reward, D and the 33-comparison adjustment, and the panel settings (trainers, budgets and timeouts). Their run counts, pools, seeds, hardware, wall times, rank correlations and seed-half references are recomputed here (panels.*) | Raw records are not in the results directory (cluster storage). Their per-cell scores, specifications and frozen reports are in panels.zip of the dataset, and scripts/review_panels.py recomputes every statistic in those reports, including the intervals with the reports' seeds and the top-10 overlaps. D and the 33-comparison adjustment come from a separate analysis script that is not released. |
| Structure appendix beyond in-sample fits: leave-one-out R^2 (M2 0.40, M1 0.07, height 0.37; on the 72 M1 0.08, M2 0.25, height 0.10), the leave-one-out Spearman rho of M2 (0.65) and its p-value, leave-one-family-out rho with intervals and held-out R^2 (M2 0.12, -0.25, 0.41; height 0.33, -0.06, 0.55; M2 on the 72 -0.17, -0.11, -0.60), the nine-fold crossfit (median rho 0.51 against 0.30; 8 of 9 folds on the 72) and the non-evolved transfer test (0.41 [0.18, 0.60] overall, 0.06 [-0.29, 0.38] within the evolved height range of 43 bodies) | scripts/structural_feature_regression.py and the authors' independent verification scripts (not released). |
| Other planned tests in the register: predicting worst-case reward (R^2 = 0.06), the rule x time-limit factorial (F(2,505) = 3.72, p = 0.025; median shifts -0.38 [-0.67, -0.18], -0.02 [-0.17, 0.16] and +0.05 [-0.13, 0.13]; rank correlations 0.71 and 0.94), contact-based termination (per-run p = 1.4e-4, rho = 0.19 [-0.18, 0.50]), the boundary-duration sweep (rho 0.92 [0.78, 0.97] and 0.85 [0.68, 0.94]; slope 0.099 [0.053, 0.150] per tenfold limit), existing-robots panel (2 of 4 robots passed the quality check) | Their recorded analyses: records/{nems_robust_selector_analysis,factorial_analysis,alt_termination_analysis,boundary_sweep_analysis}.json and records/menagerie_panel_report.md. |
| SAC scale check and body choice: the check run on 8 bodies (the plan specified 1) that failed on 4 (Standard to Reset-free ratios 2.75 to 12.84 against a planned 0.5 to 2), its replacement by agreement with PPO (6 of 7 within a factor of 2), the 120 runs of the second batch, and the earlier mechanism study's choice of the 24 bodies (8 that fell often, 8 that barely moved, 8 unaffected) | records/sac_full_panel_launch_log.md and records/sac_full_panel_spec.md (scale check, replacement and batches); scripts/mechanism_morphologies.txt lists the 24 bodies without their categories. The pilot statistics and the seed-averaged and permutation p-values are recomputed (sac.pilot.*, sac.*.seedmean_*, sac.*.perm_p). |
| Fall rules in public code (DERL's ratio 0.5 and 0.2 m clearance with a limit bootstrapped at 1,000 steps; MetaMorph's 0.3; Transform2Act's 0.7 m, 20 and 60 degree limits and alive bonuses 1.0 and 0.1; BodyGen; Evolution Gym), the Gymnasium healthy ranges (Ant [0.2, 1.0] m below its 0.25 m torso radius, Hopper above 0.7 m, Walker2d [0.8, 2.0] m, Humanoid [1.0, 2.0] m, alive bonus 1.0 and 5.0) and their fractions of the initial torso height (0.27, 0.56, 0.64, 0.71), DERL budget facts, compute hardware of the UNIMAL panels, the source of the 100 bodies (MetaMorph's training set, chosen from the 300 bodies that DERL evolved, 100 in each of three environments) and the page of DERL's statement on early termination | Source code and launch records, not run results: records/termination_rules_public_code.md (DERL, MetaMorph, Transform2Act, BodyGen, Evolution Gym) and the Gymnasium 1.0.0 MuJoCo environment files (healthy_z_range and initial torso heights of the v4 and v5 environments, not copied into the release). The body source is stated in MetaMorph (Gupta et al., ICLR 2022, Appendix A.2) and DERL (Gupta et al., Nature Communications 12, 5721, 2021, Methods). |
| Training, evaluation and probe settings: the PPO preset (5M steps as 2,441 updates of 2,048 steps, 128 environments, 16 steps per rollout, minibatch 256, 10 epochs, learning rate 3e-4, discount 0.99, GAE 0.95, clip 0.2, value and entropy coefficients 0.5 and 0.01, gradient norm 0.5, two layers of 64 units, physics step 0.005 s, frame skip 4, control step 0.02 s), the reward and reset constants (action cost 0.001, reset noise 0.01, upper height bound max(3 z0, 2 m)), 20 evaluation episodes, the SAC settings (Brax 0.14.2, 128 environments, batch 256, learning rate 3e-4, discount 0.99, reward scaling 1, replay buffer 100,000 with a minimum of 1,280, two layers of 256 units, target rate 0.005), the probe designs (drops from 1 m, 20-episode probes, 300 random sequences per body in the smaller probe) and the quantities that follow from them (about 39,000 steps per environment; about 13 and 3 episodes per environment at limits of 3,001 and 10,001 steps), and the Stable-Baselines3 PPO defaults that the preset changes (minibatch 64, entropy coefficient 0) | Launch code and trainers, not run results: corpus_revision/ (the corpus-era trainer and launchers), scripts/train_mjx.py, scripts/train_sac_mjx.py and the probe scripts in records/sim-record-scripts; the 300-sequence probe (sim-records/sk_shoot.json) was made by an independent verification script (not released). The Stable-Baselines3 defaults are those of its PPO class in version 2.3.2. |
| Launch history, dates and software versions: the launch, freeze, registration and commit dates (for example the corpus-era trainer revisions of 5 and 8 April 2026), the diagnosis of the later code in April 2026, runs per GPU, four array tasks of 25 bodies, the software of the training environment (MuJoCo and MJX 3.6.0, JAX 0.9.2) and the pinned versions of public code | Git history of the trainers and launchers (corpus_revision/ keeps both corpus-era trainer revisions and the launchers; slurm/ holds the later launchers), and the dated planned-test records in records/. |
| Decision rules and designs of the planned tests: thresholds such as rho > 0.85, median drift below 0.10, slope in [0.9, 1.1], intercept below 0.05 and 90% sign agreement (retraining), rho > 0.3 (transfer), p < 0.025 and rho >= 0.70 (longer budget), p < 0.01 and rho >= 0.60 with contact rules of 1, 50 or unlimited steps (contact termination), 0.05 PSR in 2 of 3 splits and 85% (leave-one-rule-out), upper bounds below 0.95 and partial eta^2 > 0.02 with limits of 3,001 and 10,001 steps (time limits), R^2 >= 0.20 (feature-based selector), p < 0.01 (SAC and A2C) | The frozen specifications in records/ (equivalence_pilot_report.md and the *_spec.md and *_prereg.md files). The 14 retraining sub-criteria and the SAC reliability threshold of 0.514 are recomputed (bridge.criteria_total, sac.sac.reliability_threshold). |
| The paused replication (60 non-evolved bodies, 360 runs, 309 finished, Holm-adjusted one-sided p < 0.025, about 220 GPU-hours) and the compute of the unfinished 50M-step phase of the longer-budget panel | The replication's registration and the cluster job records; neither is part of the release. |
| Crossing steps with the training reset noise (step 9 for the seven short VT cut-off bodies, steps 13 to 16 for the three taller ones) | An independent verification script and its output (not released); the zero-action records in sim-records/ use the nominal reset. |
| Statements about Monte Carlo variation between random streams: bootstrap endpoints move by about 0.01, and the upper end of the VT minus MVT interval on the 72 depends on the stream | Comparisons of this script's seeded streams with those of the authors' independent verification scripts (not released) and with the earlier summaries (the comparison section above). |
| Definitions and analysis constants printed with the results: the rule and evaluator thresholds (tau = 0, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35, 0.50), the 1,000-step limit and the 5M-step budget, seeds 42, 43 and 44, k = 5, 10 and 20, B = 10,000 and 95% intervals, the 20-step cut-off-run threshold, the 500-step survival threshold, the 5 m and 1 m walking and stopping thresholds, the 0.05 PSR floor for ratios, the 0.1 PSR diagnostic threshold, the 50-step probe criterion and the gallery percentiles | Inputs, not results: the run configurations and this script's constants and definitions (RULES, SWEEP, SEEDS, KS, BOOT_SEED, PERM_SEED and the thresholds in the row definitions). |
| A2C panel (24 of 216 cells, 48 calibration runs, p = 0.073) | The analyzed runs are lost; no A2C statistic is recomputed (disclosed in the paper). |
| Identifiers printed with the references and acknowledgments: grant numbers, DOIs, volume, issue and page numbers of the cited works, and page locators of quoted passages | The grant agreements and the bibliographic records of the cited works (publisher pages and Crossref), not results. |
